Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 1 of 13.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/migrations/0008_discord_pacing.sql\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+-- Discord pacing preferences and durable decision/event observability.\n+-- Runtime access uses service-role DB connections; RLS still denies anon by\n+-- default for defense in depth.\n+\n+ALTER TABLE users\n+    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pacing_preferences jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;\n+\n+DO $$\n+BEGIN\n+    ALTER TABLE users\n+        ADD CONSTRAINT users_pacing_preferences_object_check\n+        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(pacing_preferences) = 'object');\n+EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;\n+END $$;\n+\n+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pacing_events (\n+    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n+    user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n+    message_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',\n+    source text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'live',\n+    decision text NOT NULL CHECK (\n+        decision IN (\n+            'wait',\n+            'react',\n+            'silence',\n+            'answer',\n+            'typing_wait',\n+            'typing_start',\n+            'typing_stop',\n+            'fallback'\n+        )\n+    ),\n+    reason text NOT NULL,\n+    signal_snapshot jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n+    preference_snapshot jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n+    wait_ms integer CHECK (wait_ms IS NULL OR wait_ms >= 0),\n+    reaction text,\n+    llm_judgement jsonb,\n+    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n+);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pacing_events_user_created\n+    ON pacing_events (user_id, created_at DESC);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pacing_events_decision_created\n+    ON pacing_events (decision, created_at DESC);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pacing_events_message_ids_gin\n+    ON pacing_events USING gin (message_ids);\n+\n+ALTER TABLE pacing_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+ALTER TABLE pacing_events FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+\n+DO $$\n+BEGIN\n+    CREATE POLICY deny_anon_pacing_events ON pacing_events\n+        FOR ALL TO anon USING (false) WITH CHECK (false);\n+EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;\n+END $$;\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

   *,\n+        wait_s: float = 0.0,\n+        reaction: str | None = None,\n+    ) -> PacingDecision:\n+        return PacingDecision(\n+            action=action,\n+            reason=reason,\n+ _signal_snapshot(self, signals: list[MessageSignal], source: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+        return {\n+            \"source\": source,\n+            \"message_count\": len(signals),\n+            \"charges\": sorted({signal.charge for signal in signals}),\n+            \"has_media\": any(signal.media_type for signal in signals),\n+            \"latest_message_at\": max((signal.sent_at for Mapping[str, Any]) -> float:\n+        elapsed_s = max(0.0, (self._now() - state.first_seen_at).total_seconds())\n+        remaining_cap_s = float(preferences[\"max_typing_wait_s\"]) - elapsed_s\n+ latest_at).total_seconds())\n+        burst_window_s = float(preferences[\"burst_window_s\"])\n+        if age_s >= burst_window_s:\n+            return 0.0\n+        return min(\n+            float(preferences[\"max_wait_s\"]),\n+            max(float(preferences[\"min_wait_s\"]), burst_window_s - age_s),\n+        )\n+\n+    def _reaction_for(self, signals: list[MessageSignal], preferences: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:\n+        if not preferences[\"reactions_enabled\"]:\n+            return None\n+        if not self.settings.discord_pacing_reactions_enabled:\n+ {\"thanks\", \"thank you\", \"ty\", \"got it\", \"ok\", \"okay\", \"sounds good\", \"makes sense\"}:\n+            return ACK_REACTION\n+        if text in {\"love this\", \"i appreciate you\", _note_reaction(self, user_id: UUID) -> None:\n+        now = self._now()\n+        self._last_reaction_at[user_id] = now\n+        key = (user_id, now.date())\n+        self._reaction_counts[key] = self._reaction_counts.get(key, 0) + 1\n+\n+    def _is_low_content_ack(self, signals: list[MessageSignal]) -> bool:\n+        if len(signals) != 1 or signals[0].charge != \"routine\":\n+         Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

timedelta(seconds=10)),\n+        _seed_message(fake_pool, user, content=\"maybe just leave it\", sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=9)),\n+    ]\n+    client = _FakeMessagesClient('{\"action\":\"silence\",\"reason\":\"user is closing the loop\",\"wait_s\":0,\"reaction\":null}')\n+    pacer = DiscordPacer(fake_pool, llm_client=client, now=lambda: now)\n+\n+    decision = await pacer.decide_and_record(user, ids, source=\"live\")\n+\n+    assert decision.action == \"silence\"\n+    assert decision.llm_judgement[\"action\"] == \"silence\"\n+    assert len(client.calls) == 1\n+    assert fake_pool.llm_spend_log[\"text\"] > 0\n+    event = next(iter(fake_pool.pacing_events.values()))\n+    assert event[\"decision\"] == \"silence\"\n+    assert event[\"llm_judgement\"][\"action\"] == \"silence\"\n+\n+\n+async def test_llm_judgement_spend_cap_falls_back_without_model_call(fake_pool) -> None:\n+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)\n+    user = _seed_user(fake_pool)\n+    ids = [\n+        _seed_message(fake_pool, user, content=\"I guess\", sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=10)),\n+        _seed_message(fake_pool, user, content=\"maybe just leave it\", sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=9)),\n+    ]\n+    fake_pool.llm_spend_log[\"text\"] = Decimal(\"999\")\n+    client = _FakeMessagesClient('{\"action\":\"silence\",\"reason\":\"unused\",\"wait_s\":0,\"reaction\":null}')\n+    pacer = DiscordPacer(fake_pool, llm_client=client, now=lambda: now)\n+\n+    decision = await pacer.decide(user, ids, source=\"live\")\n+\n+    assert decision.action == \"answer\"\n+    assert len(client.calls) == 0\n+    event = next(iter(fake_pool.pacing_events.values()))\n+    assert event[\"decision\"] == \"fallback\"\n+    assert \"spend cap\" in event[\"llm_judgement\"][\"error\"]\n+\n+\n+async def test_llm_judgement_invalid_json_records_fallback(fake_pool) -> None:\n+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)\n+    user = _seed_user(fake_pool)\n+    ids = [\n+        _seed_message(fake_pool, user, content=\"I guess\", sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=10)),\n+        _seed_message(fake_pool, user, content=\"maybe just leave it\", sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=9)),\n+    ]\n+    pacer = DiscordPacer(fake_pool, llm_client=_FakeMessagesClient(\"not json\"), now=lambda: now)\n+\n+    decision = await pacer.decide(user, ids, source=\"live\")\n+\n+    assert decision.action == \"answer\"\n+    event = next(iter(fake_pool.pacing_events.values()))\n+    assert event[\"decision\"] == \"fallback\"\n+    assert \"Expecting value\" in event[\"llm_judgement\"][\"error\"]\n+\n+\n+async def test_llm_judgement_is_not_called_for_deterministic_crisis_gate(fake_pool) -> None:\n+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)\n+    user = _seed_user(fake_pool)\n+    message_id = _seed_message(\n+        fake_pool,\n+        user,\n+        content=\"I might hurt myself tonight\",\n+        charge=\"crisis\",\n+        sent_at=now - timedelta(seconds=10),\n+    )\n+    client = _FakeMessagesClient('{\"action\":\"silence\",\"reason\":\"unsafe\",\"wait_s\":0,\"reaction\":null}')\n+    pacer = DiscordPacer(fake_pool, llm_client=client, now=lambda: now)\n+\n+    decision = await pacer.decide(user, [message_id], source=\"live\")\n+\n+    assert decision.action == \"answer\"\n+    assert len(client.calls) == 0\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

decision.action == \"wait\":\n+            self._bursts[user_id] = burst\n+            self._wait_tasks[user_id] = asyncio.create_task(self._fire_after_wait(user_id, max(0.0, decision.wait_s)))\n+            return\n+        if decision.action == \"answer\":\n+            await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)\n+            return\n+        if decision.action == \"react\":\n+            if self.on_paced_reaction is not None:\n+                await self.on_paced_reaction(message_ids, burst.user, decision)\n+            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)\n+            return\n+        if decision.action == \"silence\":\n+            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)\n+            return\n+        await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)\n+\n+    async def _fire_after_wait(self, user_id: UUID, wait_s: float) -> None:\n+        try:\n+            await asyncio.sleep(wait_s)\n+            lock = self._locks.setdefault(user_id, asyncio.Lock())\n+            async with lock:\n+                self._wait_tasks.pop(user_id, None)\n+                burst = self._bursts.pop(user_id, None)\n+                if burst is not None:\n+                    await self._handle_ready_burst(user_id, burst)\n+        except asyncio.CancelledError:\n+            raise\n+\n+    async def _call_paced_answer(self, message_ids: list[UUID], user: User, decision: PacingDecision) -> None:\n+        if self.on_paced_answer is not None:\n+            await self.on_paced_answer(message_ids, user, decision)\n+            return\n+        await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, user)\n+\n+    async def _mark_processed(self, message_ids: list[UUID]) -> None:\n+        pool = getattr(self.pacer, \"pool\", None)\n+        if pool is None or not message_ids:\n+            return\n+        await pool.execute(\n+            \"UPDATE messages SET processing_state='processed' WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]) AND processing_state='raw'\",\n+            message_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    def _merge_source(self, existing: str, incoming: str) -> str:\n+        if existing == incoming:\n+            return existing\n+        if existing == \"mixed\":\n+            return existing\n+        return \"mixed\"\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

        Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs. Go deep on UX details and implement the best pragmatic version in this repo.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: docs/discord-pacing.md\n+# Discord Pacing\n+\n+Discord pacing makes Discord DMs feel less like a webhook and more like an\n+attentive conversation. It is only active when `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=discord` and\n+`DISCORD_PACING_ENABLED=true`; other providers keep the legacy coalescer and\n+outbound behavior.\n+\n+## Runtime Flow\n+\n+Inbound Discord messages still persist through the normal inbound path, then\n+enter `BurstCoalescer` with source metadata. When pacing is enabled, the\n+coalescer asks `DiscordPacer` for one of four pre-turn actions:\n+\n+| Action | Behavior |\n+| --- | --- |\n+| `wait` | Delay and reschedule the burst so nearby lines and active typing can settle. |\n+| `react` | Add one sparse allowlisted reaction and mark the messages processed without a full agentic turn. |\n+| `silence` | Mark the messages processed without replying when the burst is clearly conversationally closed. |\n+| `answer` | Run the normal agentic turn with compact pacing metadata included in the trigger context. |\n+\n+Deterministic gates always run before optional model judgement. Crisis,\n+charged, media, catch-up, and recovery work must answer; they cannot be\n+silenced or handled by reaction only.\n+\n+## Source Modes\n+\n+| Source | Entry point | Pacing behavior |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `live` | Normal Discord DM ingestion. | May wait for burst coalescing or user typing, and may use sparse reactions or silence for routine low-risk acknowledgements. |\n+| `catch_up` | Discord startup/history catch-up through `process_inbound(..., coalescer_source=\"catch_up\")`. | Treated as stale offline work, so it bypasses live typing delays, reactions, and silence. |\n+| `media` | Voice transcription and image analysis success paths. | Must answer so media-derived context is not dropped. |\n+| `recovery` | Orphan raw-message recovery after an interrupted process. | Must answer so recovered work is reconciled explicitly; crashed turn recovery still uses direct `add_burst(...)`. |\n+\n+## Typing UX\n+\n+The Discord gateway reports raw `TYPING_START` events through\n+`GatewayCallbacks.on_event`. The pacer records that runtime state and extends a\n+live burst while the user appears to be composing. `_handle_message` does not\n+start an immediate bot typing task; typing is controlled by the pacing decision.\n+\n+For paced answers, the pacer can show short human typing pulses before the\n+agentic turn, but it suppresses those pulses while the user is actively typing.\n+Agentic sends that include pacing metadata call Discord outbound delivery with\n+`send_typing_indicator=false`, avoiding a second low-level typing indicator.\n+Operational or non-paced Discord sends keep the default\n+`send_typing_indicator=true`.\n+\n+## LLM Judgement\n+\n+`DISCORD_PACING_LLM_JUDGEMENT_ENABLED` allows a small text model call only for\n+ambiguous live routine bursts above `DISCORD_PACING_LLM_MIN_AMBIGUITY`. Before\n+that call, the normal text spend cap is checked. Accepted calls are cost\n+recorded through `llm_spend_log`, decoded as strict JSON, and then validated\n+against deterministic safety and source gates. Spend-cap skips, invalid JSON,\n+model failures, and policy overrides are recorded in `pacing_events` as\n+fallbacks.\n+\n+## Observability\n+\n+Every pacer decision records a `pacing_events` row with:\n+\n+- `user_id`\n+- `message_ids`\n+- `source`\n+- `decision`\n+- `reason`\n+- `signal_snapshot`\n+- `preference_snapshot`\n+- `wait_ms`\n+- `reaction`\n+- `llm_judgement`\n+\n+Use these rows to answer why a burst waited, reacted, silenced, or answered.\n+`DISCORD_PACING_EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS` is the operator retention target for this\n+audit stream.\n+\n+## Global Settings\n+\n+| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_ENABLED` | `true` | Enables Discord pacing when the provider is Discord. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_BURST_WINDOW_S` | `2.75` | Base window for coalescing nearby messages. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_MIN_WAIT_S` | `0.8` | Lower bound for wait decisions. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_MAX_WAIT_S` | `12` | Upper bound for ordinary wait decisions. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_TYPING_GRACE_S` | `4` | How recently a user typing event counts as active composition. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_TYPING_EXTEND_S` | `2` | Extra wait added while the user appears to be typing. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_MAX_TYPING_WAIT_S` | `20` | Maximum typing-driven wait budget. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_ANSWER_TYPING_MIN_S` | `1` | Minimum pre-answer typing pulse duration. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_ANSWER_TYPING_MAX_S` | `10` | Maximum pre-answer typing pulse duration. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_ANSWER_CHARS_PER_S` | `18` | Approximate answer typing speed used to size pulses. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_REACTIONS_ENABLED` | `true` | Enables sparse reaction-only decisions. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_REACTION_COOLDOWN_S` | `180` | Minimum gap between reaction-only decisions for a user. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_REACTION_DAILY_LIMIT` | `12` | Per-user daily cap for reaction-only decisions. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_SILENCE_COOLDOWN_S` | `300` | Minimum gap before another silence decision for a user. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_LLM_JUDGEMENT_ENABLED` | `true` | Enables optional model judgement for ambiguous live routine bursts. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_LLM_MIN_AMBIGUITY` | `0.45` | Ambiguity threshold before the model can be consulted. |\n+| `DISCORD_PACING_EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS` | `30` | Target retention window for decision audit rows. |\n+\n+## Per-User Preferences\n+\n+Per-user overrides live in `users.pacing_preferences`. They are always clamped\n+before storage or use, so malformed JSON cannot create unbounded sleeps or turn\n+off deterministic safety gates.\n+\n+| Key | Bounds | Notes |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `enabled` | Boolean | Turns pacing on or off for the user, defaulting to the global setting. |\n+| `burst_window_s` | `0.25` to `min(max_wait_s, 15.0)` | How long to coalesce nearby lines. |\n+| `min_wait_s` | `0.0` to `min(global max wait, 10.0)` | Clamped not to exceed `max_wait_s`. |\n+| `max_wait_s` | `max(global min wait, 1.0)` to `60.0` | Caps ordinary waits. |\n+| `typing_grace_s` | `0.5` to `30.0` | How long a typing signal remains fresh. |\n+| `max_typing_wait_s` | `1.0` to `90.0` | Caps typing-driven extension. |\n+| `answer_typing_min_s` | `0.0` to `20.0` | Clamped not to exceed `answer_typing_max_s`. |\n+| `answer_typing_max_s` | `0.5` to `45.0` | Caps pre-answer typing pulses. |\n+| `answer_chars_per_s` | `4.0` to `80.0` | Slower values make longer answer typing pulses. |\n+| `reactions_enabled` | Boolean | Allows reaction-only decisions for that user. |\n+| `reaction_daily_limit` | `0` to `100` | Per-user daily reaction-only cap. |\n+\n+Example override:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"burst_window_s\": 4,\n+  \"typing_grace_s\": 6,\n+  \"reactions_enabled\": false,\n+  \"answer_chars_per_s\": 14\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## Operator Tuning\n+\n+Tune globals first, then use per-user preferences only for stable individual\n+needs. Keep reaction limits low enough that reactions feel intentional. Prefer\n+raising `DISCORD_PACING_BURST_WINDOW_S` before raising maximum waits if users\n+often send multi-line thoughts. Validate final timing in a real Discord DM after\n+deploy, because client typing indicators and subjective pacing cannot be fully\n+proven by unit tests.\n*** Update File: README.md\n@@\n - `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` when using `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=discord`\n - `DISCORD_PARTNER_USER_ID_A` and `DISCORD_PARTNER_USER_ID_B` when using `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=discord`\n - `DISCORD_PARTNER_NAME_A` and `DISCORD_PARTNER_NAME_B` when using `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=discord`\n+- `DISCORD_PACING_*` variables when using Discord conversation pacing\n - `ADMIN_PASSWORD`\n@@\n-for file in migrations/0001_init.sql migrations/0002_plan2.sql migrations/0003_plan4_oob_reviews.sql migrations/0004_plan5_scheduled_jobs.sql migrations/0005_plan6_ops.sql migrations/0006_plan7_eval_results.sql migrations/0007_security_hardening.sql; do\n+for file in migrations/0001_init.sql migrations/0002_plan2.sql migrations/0003_plan4_oob_reviews.sql migrations/0004_plan5_scheduled_jobs.sql migrations/0005_plan6_ops.sql migrations/0006_plan7_eval_results.sql migrations/0007_security_hardening.sql migrations/0008_discord_pacing.sql; do\n@@\n-It creates 11 spec tables plus the operational `llm_spend_log` table.         Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs.         Idea:
Implement a human-like Discord conversation pacing system for Veas. It should combine deterministic programmatic signals and LLM judgement so the bot feels like a real attentive person rather than message-in/reply-out. Requirements: handle Discord typing signals and pause/extend while user is composing; coalesce multi-message bursts and respond to the whole thought rather than every line; support wait/react/silence/answer decisions before the full agentic turn; use tasteful/elegant emoji/reaction behavior sparingly; avoid over-questioning and compulsive replies; preserve safety/OOB behavior; make typing indicators feel human, including possible stop/start and not typing while the user is typing; add observability/reasoning for why the pacer waited/reacted/silenced/answered; make timing configurable and per-user preference friendly; add tests and docs.
